# Application Storage SQL and CQL performance review

The inventory covers **980 query and command definitions across 14 DbContext folders**, plus **1066 top-level schema and migration statements**. It includes the inline event-log binary COPY and the shared table-readability query. Parameterized templates are listed once per definition. The SQL-shape column names the top-level operation and source or target where present. Schema functions and DO blocks are listed as single definitions.

This is a static review of the checked-in SQL, CQL, caller paths and schema. Live plans, row counts, partition sizes, call frequency and latency were unavailable. **Keep** means the source shows a suitable access pattern. **Measure** marks workload-dependent candidates and checks. **Maintenance** denotes setup, recovery, or migration work. **Change first** identifies a concrete rewrite to validate.

## Highest-value candidates

- **TradeTickQuoteDb**. Replace the two legacy latest-ID aggregates with descending clustering reads and LIMIT 1. The full-row latest methods already use that order. [GetLastFuturesTickDataId](<C:/repos/IFM/TomasAI.IFM.Application.Storage/TradeTickQuoteDb/TradeTickQuoteDbSql.cs:168>)
- **TradePlanDb**. Use revision keysets for sequential history navigation. The offset path skips rows and repeats an exact count for each opened page. [OffsetHistoryRowsTemplate](<C:/repos/IFM/TomasAI.IFM.Application.Storage/TradePlanDb/TradePlanDbSql.cs:64>)
- **EventSourceDb**. Measure claiming under a large backlog. Existing eligibility indexes omit the projector key and cannot supply the complete claim order. [ClaimEventProjectorOutbox](<C:/repos/IFM/TomasAI.IFM.Application.Storage/EventSourceDb/EventSourceActorDbSql.cs:59>)
- **MarketDataDb**. Benchmark ordered seeks for latest daily ADX, ATR, MACD and RSI. Existing indexes put timestamp ahead of valuedate. [GetLastFuturesRsiDailySignal](<C:/repos/IFM/TomasAI.IFM.Application.Storage/MarketDataDb/MarketDataDbSql.cs:760>)
- **SecuritiesDb**. Test an index matching ascending contract month and the explicit C collations of the page cursor. [GetNativeOptionContractsPage](<C:/repos/IFM/TomasAI.IFM.Application.Storage/SecuritiesDb/SecuritiesDbSql.cs:287>)
- **TradeDb**. Test an index matching the portfolio, fund, date, order and revision ordering before fetching execution payloads. [ReadFundOrderFills](<C:/repos/IFM/TomasAI.IFM.Application.Storage/TradeDb/TradeDbSql.cs:2337>)
- **MarketDataServiceDb**. Test a date and cursor index matching the recovery-log order and optional dataset filter. [ReadRecoveryLog](<C:/repos/IFM/TomasAI.IFM.Application.Storage/MarketDataServiceDb/MarketDataServiceDbSql.cs:242>)

Nine checked-in PostgreSQL secondary indexes repeat the same keys and order as a primary key. Their rows in the schema inventory identify the matching constraint. Removing a verified duplicate can reduce index maintenance on writes. Check index dependencies, deployed definitions and usage first.

## ConfigurationDb

PostgreSQL SQL. 88 definitions and 338 schema or migration statements.

GetStrategyCatalogTemplate renders correlated JSON aggregate child reads from StrategyCatalogChildJson. The owner key scopes each child query. Profile child cardinality and serialization for large catalogs. [StrategyCatalogChildJson](<C:/repos/IFM/TomasAI.IFM.Application.Storage/ConfigurationDb/ConfigurationDbContextExtensions.cs:176>)

## MarketDataDb

PostgreSQL SQL. 185 definitions and 185 schema or migration statements.

The latest-signal indexes should be considered as a family. Several existing definitions put timestamp before the date and repeat timestamp later. Compare write cost and existing query coverage before replacing indexes. Keep the named canonical indexes required by GetCanonicalQueryReadiness.

PostgreSQL SQL. 5 definitions and 6 schema or migration statements.

## TradeTickQuoteDb

ScyllaDB CQL. 25 definitions and 14 schema or migration statements.

Raw trade and quote tables partition on (asset_type_id, contract_id). Date is a clustering key. Date bucketing would change multi-day range reads and require ordered fan-out or a latest-by-contract read model. The legacy futures_tick_data_by_time table already partitions by contract and date. The six raw range definitions and GetFuturesTickDataByDate have no caller in this project, so they are lower-priority unless a consumer is identified.

| Definition | SQL shape | Assessment | Performance finding |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

Bulk purges can increase tombstone reads. Profile churn and keep the by-time delete paired for futures. |
| [DeleteFuturesTickData](<C:/repos/IFM/TomasAI.IFM.Application.Storage/TradeTickQuoteDb/TradeTickQuoteDbSql.cs:14>) | DELETE `futures_tick_data` | Measure | Contract partition with a date clustering slice. Bulk purges can increase tombstone reads. Profile churn and keep the by-time delete paired for futures. |

## Runtime validation

- Rank PostgreSQL candidates by calls, execution time and I/O using pg_stat_statements where available. Compare EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) for selective and broad parameters on representative data. Execute write plans in an isolated fixture. [pg_stat_statements](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgstatstatements.html) · [Using EXPLAIN](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/using-explain.html)
- Compare sort input, filtered rows, payload size, write amplification and lock waits before adding indexes. Verify leading keys, ordering and collation. [Multicolumn indexes](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes-multicolumn.html) · [Indexes and ORDER BY](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes-ordering.html)
- For ScyllaDB, compare traced reads, bytes, partition size, tombstones and client p95/p99 for typical and large partitions. Measure ingest changes with concurrent readers. [ScyllaDB SELECT](https://docs.scylladb.com/manual/stable/cql/dml/select.html) · [ScyllaDB BATCH](https://docs.scylladb.com/manual/stable/cql/dml/batch.html)
- Measure end-to-end round trips and payload decoding. The PostgreSQL provider already uses NpgsqlBatch for queued operations and the ScyllaDB provider caches prepared statements. [Npgsql performance](https://www.npgsql.org/doc/performance.html)
